=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minSpeedOnTime(self, dist: List[int], hour: float) -> int:
        if len(dist) >= hour - 1  : return -1
        left,right = 1, ceil(max(max(dist),dist[-2]/(1 if hour.is_integer() else hour-int(hour))))
        while left<right:
            mid=(left+right)//2
            if sum([ceil(i/mid) for i in dist[:-1]])+(dist[-1]/mid) <= hour :
                right=mid
            else:
                left=mid+1
        return left

Let's craft fixed code:

```python
from typing import List
from math import ceil

class Solution:
    def minSpeedOnTime(self, dist: List[int], hour: float) -> int:
        n = len(dist)
        if hour <= n - 1:
            return -1

        # For n == 1, this formula still works:
        # right = ceil(dist[0] / hour)
        right = max(
            max(dist[:-1]) if n > 1 else 0,
            ceil(dist[-1] / (hour - (n - 1)))
        )
        left = 1

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            time = sum((d + mid - 1) // mid for d in dist[:-1])
            time += dist[-1] / mid
            if time <= hour:
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1
        return left
```

Let's write final code:

```python
from typing import List
from math import ceil

class Solution:
    def minSpeedOnTime(self, dist: List[int], hour: float) -> int:
        n = len(dist)
        if hour <= n - 1:
            return -1

        right = max(
            max(dist[:-1]) if n > 1 else 0,
            ceil(dist[-1] / (hour - (n - 1)))
        )
        left = 1

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            total = 0
            for i in range(n - 1):
                total += (dist[i] + mid - 1) // mid
            total += dist[-1] / mid

            if total <= hour:
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return left
```

against the original code and the task. The user asked to fix the faulty code and output only the fixed code. The final answer should be just code. I need to ensure no explanation. The code I plan to output:

```python
from typing import List
from math import ceil

class Solution:
    def minSpeedOnTime(self, dist: List[int], hour: float) -> int:
        n = len(dist)
        if hour <= n - 1:
            return -1

        right = max(
            max(dist[:-1]) if n > 1 else 0,
            ceil(dist[-1] / (hour - (n - 1)))
        )
        left = 1

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            total = 0
            for i in range(n - 1):
                total += (dist[i] + mid - 1) // mid
            total += dist[-1] / mid

            if total <= hour:
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return left
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from math import ceil

class Solution:
    def minSpeedOnTime(self, dist: List[int], hour: float) -> int:
        n = len(dist)
        if hour <= n - 1:
            return -1

        right = max(
            max(dist[:-1]) if n > 1 else 0,
            ceil(dist[-1] / (hour - (n - 1)))
        )
        left = 1

        while left < right:
            mid = (left + right) // 2
            total = 0
            for i in range(n - 1):
                total += (dist[i] + mid - 1) // mid
            total += dist[-1] / mid

            if total <= hour:
                right = mid
            else:
                left = mid + 1

        return left
